### 📱 1. Mobile Responsiveness Optimization
- **Header & Navbar**: Fixed navbar height (`64px`) and scaled the logo nicely on small screens without overlapping elements.
- **Hero & Typography**: Dynamic typography with fluid clamp sizing to ensure titles and subheadings fit smaller viewports cleanly.
- **Hero Buttons**: Converted CTA buttons to full width on mobile (`<= 480px`) for easier touch interaction.
- **Card Grids & Paddings**: Optimized package cards, service cards, why-us items, and estimator form paddings (`20px`) for seamless mobile scrolling.
- **Modals & Actions**: Enhanced package details & lead dashboard modals to scale within mobile viewports (`max-height: 94vh`) with stacked action buttons on narrow screens.
- **Floating WhatsApp FAB**: Fixed bottom positioning (`bottom: 20px; right: 16px`) so it does not cover modal or form action buttons.

All requested updates have been implemented and deployed live!

### 🌐 Live Production Link:
👉 **[https://cholojai-eight.vercel.app](https://cholojai-eight.vercel.app)**

### ✨ Summary of Improvements:

1. **📱 Budget Calculator Mobile Layout Redesign**:
   - **Form Container**: Wrapped select dropdowns and addon checkboxes in flexible full-width containers with refined padding (`18px-24px`) to prevent text overflowing or horizontal scrolling on small screens.
   - **Select Inputs**: Enabled proper text wrapping and responsive font size (`13px`) for long destination titles on mobile screens.
   - **Summary Card**: Optimized total text block (`Ask on WhatsApp`), table rows, and button widths for clean single-column presentation on smartphones.

2. **🇧🇩 Added 10 Top Bangladeshi Tourist Spots Showcase**:
   - Integrated a dedicated **"Top Tourist Destinations in Bangladesh"** showcase section (`#bd-destinations`) featuring:
     - ☁️ **Sajek Valley** (Rangamati)
     - ⛰️ **Bandarban** (Chittagong Hill Tracts)
     - 🏖️ **Cox's Bazar** (Chittagong Division)
     - 🏝️ **St. Martin's Island** (Bay of Bengal)
     - 🐅 **Sundarbans** (Khulna Division)
     - 🌊 **Tanguar Haor** (Sunamganj, Sylhet)
     - 🍃 **Sreemangal** (Moulvibazar, Sylhet)
     - 🌉 **Rangamati** (Kaptai Lake)
     - 🌅 **Kuakata** (Patuakhali District)
     - 🏕️ **Khagrachari** (Chittagong Hill Tracts)
   - Added a **"BD Spots"** quick link to both the top navigation bar and mobile drawer.

3. **🛡️ Admin Removal**:
   - Removed the `Admin` button from the website footer.

4. **🚀 Git & Vercel Deployment**:
   - Committed changes (`d37fb67`) to GitHub.
   - Deployed live to Vercel production (`dpl_D3pPe6tkid6JvCW44hHXkE4YX7NR`).

---

### 🏢 1. New Corporate Tour & Lead Generation Section (`#corporate`):
- **B2B Service Showcase**:
  - 💼 **Corporate Retreats & Annual Picnics**: Customized turn-key retreats in Cox's Bazar, Sajek, Bandarban, Sundarban & luxury resorts.
  - 🤝 **MICE & International Conferences**: Venue booking, flight tickets, hotel reservations, transfers & visa coordination for expos in Dubai, Thailand & Singapore.
  - 🧾 **B2B Terms & Invoicing**: Corporate credit terms, GST invoicing, group rates & 24/7 dedicated account manager.
- **Dedicated B2B Proposal Form**:
  - Fields for **Company Name**, **Contact Person**, **Corporate WhatsApp**, **Destination Preference**, and **Group Size (10 to 100+ employees)**.
  - Form submissions automatically generate structured B2B lead inquiries on WhatsApp & record leads in your local database.

---

### 🌏 2. Expanded Core Travel Offerings (`#services`):
- ✈️ **Foreign Travel Packages**: Highlighted destinations (Thailand, Dubai, Malaysia, Singapore, Bali, Kashmir, Nepal, Sri Lanka, Vietnam).
- 🛂 **Express Visa Assistance**: 99.4% success rate for India IVAC, Thailand E-Visa, UAE, UK, USA & Schengen.
- 🚢 **Luxury River & Sea Cruises**: Tanguar Haor luxury houseboats, Sundarban mangrove cruises, St. Martin bay ships & Dubai Marina dinner cruises.
- 🎫 **Air Ticket Reservation**: Instant booking & re-issuance (Biman, US-Bangla, AirAsia, Emirates, Qatar, Saudia).
- 🏨 **5-Star Hotel & Resort Booking**: Pre-negotiated rates for beachfront resorts, cloud cottages & city hotels.
- 🕋 **Executive Umrah & Hajj**: 5-Star Makkah & Madinah packages near the Harams with visa & private Ziyarat.

---

- Pushed commit `cff05b6` to GitHub `origin/main`.
- Live deployment ready on Vercel (`dpl_D8WuevNcqRTTyiSHFXnuRAPCEbpt`).

### ✨ Summary of Fixes & Enhancements:

1. **☀️ Light Theme Header & Contrast Fix**:
   - **Header Background**: Configured dynamic background variable `--nav-bg` (`rgba(255,255,255, 0.92)`) so the navbar smoothly transitions to a clean crisp white background when switching to Light Mode.
   - **Mobile Drawer**: Updated mobile drawer background `--nav-bg-scrolled` to match Light/Dark mode seamlessly.
   - **Form Select Options & Inputs**: Fixed `<select>` dropdown option colors in light mode so options render with sharp dark text (`#0f172a`) on white backgrounds.
   - **Theme Toggle Button**: Upgraded icon button container styling and contrast for clear visibility in both light and dark themes.

2. **📸 Real Destination Photos Added**:
   - 🏞️ **Tanguar Haor Luxury Houseboat**: Real photo of luxury houseboat cruising past Meghalaya mountain backdrops.
   - 🐅 **Sundarbans Forest Cruise**: Real photo of mangrove safari boat with deer on the riverbanks.
   - 🏝️ **Maldives Overwater Resort**: Added luxury bungalow resort villa photography.
   - All 8 tour package cards now feature real photography for Cox's Bazar, Sajek, Kashmir, Dubai, Thailand, Umrah, Tanguar Haor, and Sundarban.

3. **🔤 Typography & Font Polish**:
   - Configured crisp typographic hierarchy with Google Fonts **Outfit** for bold headings (`font-weight: 700-900`) and **Inter** for clean, readable body copy across all screen sizes.

3. **🔤 Upgraded to Premium Typography**:

### ✨ Form Fixes & Enhancements:

1. **🏷️ Visual Icon Labels**:
   - Added parrot green icons to every input label (🏢 Company Name, 👤 Contact Person, 💬 Corporate WhatsApp, 📍 Destination Preference, 👥 Group Size).

2. **📝 Special Requirements Textarea**:

3. **💬 Structured WhatsApp Payload**:


8. 🌉 **Rangamati**: Real photo of Kaptai Lake hanging bridge and colorful wooden boats (`images/spot_rangamati.jpg`).
9. 🌅 **Kuakata**: Real photo of golden hour ocean sunrise beach with traditional wooden fishing boats (`images/spot_kuakata.jpg`).
10. 🏕️ **Khagrachari**: Real photo of Risang Waterfall cascading down lush tropical forest rocks (`images/spot_khagrachari.jpg`).

### ✨ Summary of Updates:

1. **📸 Real Photos Added to "Visa, Flights & More" Cards (`#services`)**:
